## HYP-2004: harmonic covering proves LRC n=3 and isolates higher resonance debt (oracle-2026-06-01-S526)
**Status:** PARTIALLY-TRUE; n=3 proved, n>=4 reduced to resonance correction bounds.
**What:** In the covering formulation, blocking arcs `B_s={t: ||st||<1/n}` have measure `2/n`, and LRC is `SAFE != empty`. Fourier expansion gives `|SAFE| = (1-2/n)^(n-1) + resonance corrections`. For n=3, two-runner resonances factor through the mod-3 Legendre character, yielding `|SAFE| = 1/9 + (2/9)chi(a)chi(b)/(ab) >= 0`, with equality only for `{1,2}` and boundary witness `t=1/3`.
**Evidence:** `lrc_small_n_covering_proof_s526.py` verifies the formula against numerics and scans n=4..7 with `0` failures; AP is always tight but not uniquely tight at n=5,6.
**Predictions:** Closing n=4 requires bounding the first 3-term resonance correction; a general lower bound saying total resonance correction is at least `-(1-2/n)^(n-1)` is exactly LRC in harmonic-covering language.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2004-lrc-covering-harmonic-n3-proof.md`, `04-computation/lrc_small_n_covering_proof_s526.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_small_n_covering_proof_s526.out`, `07-reflections/lrc-small-n-covering-proof-s526.md`, HYP-2003, HYP-1998, THM-381.

## HYP-2005: center-grid geometry proves LRC n=3; codimension grows with n (oracle-2026-06-01-S522o)
**Status:** PROVED for n=3 (geometric); n=4 frontier mapped. Validates the S521o methodology.
**What:** Using only the geodesic/box methodology: n=3 PROVEN -- scaling-invariance gives gcd(v1,v2)=1, 0<v1<v2; for v2>=3 the times `t_k=(2k+1)/(2v2)` put runner2 at its far-center (`||v2 t_k||=1/2`) and `{||v1 t_k||}` are `v2` equally-spaced points (spacing `1/v2<=1/3`) so one meets the far-band `[1/3,2/3]`; for `v2<=2` it is `{1,2}`, `t=1/3`. n=4: a single center-grid witness covers `1275/1336`, center-grid plus n-gon covers `1299/1336`, and the residual `37` are `{1,k,k+1}` resonant triples, all lonely. The codimension grows by one per runner, making n=14 the high-codimension version on resonant near-extremal families.
**Evidence:** `lrc_geometric_small_n_s522o.py` (+ .out): n=3 `1101/1101`; n=4 `1275/1299` simple witnesses, `37` residual, `0` counterexamples.
**Predictions:** An iterated/multi-scale grid or per-cell lonely-interval method closes n=4,5; residual difficulty should concentrate on resonant `{1,...}` families at each n; grid-vs-box codimension is the quantitative obstruction.
**See:** `07-reflections/lrc-geometric-proof-n3-and-n4-frontier-s522o.md`, HYP-2002, HYP-2000, THM-369.

## HYP-2006: machine-checked LRC cases (all-n no-multiple, n=2, n=3 sieve cover) in Lean (oracle-2026-06-01-S528)
**Status:** PROVED (machine-checked, axiom-clean). Formalization milestone extending THM-369.
**What:** Three Lean 4 / Mathlib lemmas added to `LonelyRunner.lean`, each `#print axioms`-clean (`[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`, no sorry, no project axiom): (1) `lonely_of_no_multiple` -- for EVERY n>0, if no speed is divisible by n then t=1/n is n-lonely (the n-gon-vertex witness, all-n unconditional fragment); (2) `lonely_two` -- full n=2 via t=1/(2a) through `far_iff_fract`; (3) `three_lonely_sieve_cover` -- n=3 holds if no speed div by 3 OR no speed div by 2, isolating in checked code the residual kernel (speeds entangled with 6 after gcd-reduction).
**S534 extension:** THM-388 adds `three_one_three_mul_lonely`, proving in Lean that every residual family `{1,3r}` has the explicit `3`-lonely witness `t=1/3+1/(9r)`. The theorem is axiom-clean and pushes the formalization beyond the pure denominator sieve.
**Honest boundary:** the residual n=3 kernel (some speed div 3 AND some div 2) is NOT yet formalized; it is closed on paper by S522o (center-grid 1-D pigeonhole) and S526 (mod-3 Legendre character sum, |SAFE|=1/9+(2/9)chi(a)chi(b)/(ab)). The Lean file now faithfully separates "proved by sieve" from "needs the hard character/grid bound".
**Predictions/next:** formalizing the 1-D equally-spaced-points pigeonhole (S522o) would close n=3 completely in Lean; the mod-3 Gauss-sum evaluation (S526) is the next rung; the higher-resonance bound shared by n>=4 and n=14 is the research-grade rung.
**See:** `07-reflections/lrc-lean-formalized-cases-all-n-and-n3-sieve-cover-s528.md`, `07-reflections/lrc-lean-small-residual-family-s534.md`, `04-computation/lean/TournamentH7/TournamentH7/LonelyRunner.lean`, THM-369, THM-386, THM-388, HYP-2004, HYP-2005.

## HYP-2007: regular-polygon outside/inside split organizes LRC; inside-diagonal (r>=3) debt is the obstruction, born at n=4 (oracle-2026-06-01-S529)
**Status:** SUPPORTED (computation + identities); reframes the LRC obstruction geometrically.
**What:** The maximally symmetric tournament is the regular-n-gon (rotational/QR) one. Its chords split by dihedral skip-shell: skip-1 = SIDES (outside) = Hamiltonian cycle = ranking/cut/base-path; skip>=2 = DIAGONALS (inside) = cyclic/tile/cycle space. Cyclotomic chord-product prod|1-w^k|=n (verified n=3..10) ties the threshold 1/n to the polygon's OWN nearest-neighbour gap; for prime n the inside balance is the Gauss sum |sum chi(k)w^k|=sqrt(n) (verified p=3..13). The covering measure |LONELY(v)| = sum over resonances (sum m_i v_i=0) of prod f-hat(m_i), graded by order r=#nonzero m_i: r=0 = (1-2/n)^{n-1} (outside/independence), r=2 = pairwise (= S526's Legendre 1/9+(2/9)chi(a)chi(b)/(ab) for n=3, reproduced exactly), r>=3 = INSIDE DEBT (deep diagonals = S526/S527 higher-resonance obstruction).
**Evidence (lrc_polygon_inside_outside_s529.py +.out):** every regular-polygon AP set (1..n-1) gives direct |LONELY|=0 EXACTLY at n=3,4,5 (outside+inside annihilate; the Gauss-sqrt(n) balance in measure form = the unique tight case). Inside debt (r>=3) is identically 0 for n=3 (triangle has NO diagonals; two speeds admit no 3-term resonance) and first switches on at n=4 (-0.081 AP) -- the geometric birth of the open case.
**Predictions:** LRC = "signed sum of inside-diagonal shell contributions >= -(1-2/n)^{n-1}, equality only at regular polygon"; reduces to Gauss/Kloosterman sqrt(n)-domination per dihedral shell. Prime n classical (Weil); composite/AP frontiers (n=14,16,18) hard because shells share moduli (cf S17 "if 15 were prime").
**See:** `07-reflections/polygon-outside-sides-inside-diagonals-and-lrc-resonance-debt-s529.md`, `04-computation/lrc_polygon_inside_outside_s529.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_polygon_inside_outside_s529.out`, HYP-2004, HYP-2005, HYP-2006, THM-369, THM-386.

## HYP-2008: the apex (source-sink) arc closes the polygon, is the unique boundary tile, and is the LRC loneliness gap (oracle-2026-06-01-S530)
**Status:** SUPPORTED/PROVED combinatorially (verified n=3..8); refines HYP-2007/S529.
**What:** Refinement of S529 "outside = base path." The polygon boundary is a Hamiltonian CYCLE (n arcs); the base path is only n-1. The closing arc = the source-sink arc (vertex n top-of-ranking to vertex 1 bottom) = the apex tile (n,1), range x-y=n-1 (MAXIMAL tile, staircase apex). So OUTSIDE (n sides) = base path (n-1) + apex (1); INSIDE diagonals = all OTHER tiles = C(n-1,2)-1 = n(n-3)/2. The apex is (a) the UNIQUE tile on the boundary (cyclic skip 1; all others skip>=2 = interior diagonals); (b) the cut/cycle HINGE -- its fundamental cycle is the WHOLE n-gon (others span contiguous (x-y+1)-sub-polygons); (c) the reason n=3 has no inside debt (its ONLY tile IS the apex, on the boundary; inside born at n=4 with diagonals (3,1),(4,2)).
**LRC:** rooting the cyclic order at the observer, source=leader, sink=trailer, source-sink arc = largest wrap-around gap = the arc cut to linearize into the ranking path = the OBSERVER'S BRACKETING GAP. Observer-lonely <=> apex clears 1/n on both sides (a>=1/n,b>=1/n). The n circle-arcs (n-1 inter-runner = runner base-path + 1 observer-apex) sum to 1; loneliness decided entirely by the apex. Regular polygon: apex clears exactly 1/n (tight). LRC = "the source-sink (apex) arc can always be opened."
**Evidence (lrc_apex_arc_source_sink_s530.py +.out):** dictionary verified n=3..8 (apex always (n,1), range n-1, unique boundary tile, inside=n(n-3)/2, apex fund-cycle=n); lonely-time scans n=3,4,5 show apex-gap loneliness, AP=regular gives clearance exactly 1/n.
**Predictions:** track the DYNAMIC apex (bracketing runner-pair changes at wall-crossings) as an interval-exchange on the source-sink arc; bounded-ansatz (S514/S519) = "apex cannot stay below 1/n-clearance for a full lap."
**CONVERGENT (two oracle S530 sessions independently hit this).** The parallel S530 (`07-reflections/the-source-sink-arc-is-the-pivot-s530.md`, `source_sink_apex_arc_s530.py`) adds three further guises of the SAME arc: (d) flipping the apex on the transitive tournament gives the MAXIMAL single-tile jump H = 1 + 2^{n-2} (n=3..8: 3,5,9,17,33,65) = transitive's big SC neighbour on the principal line; (e) it is the #SCC switch between block-extremes -- transitive (#SCC=n, largest gap>1/2, semicircle) <-> strong (#SCC=1, largest gap<1/2, Moon), 0 mismatches n=4,5,6; (f) it selects the two LRC lonely regimes (observer-outside/semicircle vs observer-inside/wrap). Combined statement: one arc = boundary closer = staircase apex/hypotenuse = max H-jump 1+2^{n-2} = #SCC n<->1 switch = LRC observer loneliness gap.
**See:** `07-reflections/the-apex-arc-source-sink-closes-the-polygon-and-is-the-lrc-gap-s530.md` (this session: combinatorial dictionary + gap-sum verification), `07-reflections/the-source-sink-arc-is-the-pivot-s530.md` (parallel: H-jump / #SCC / regimes), `04-computation/lrc_apex_arc_source_sink_s530.py`, `04-computation/source_sink_apex_arc_s530.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_apex_arc_source_sink_s530.out`, HYP-2007, HYP-2004, THM-374, THM-382, THM-386.

## HYP-2009: LRC is outside clasp-gap forcing plus hidden inside chord debt (codex-2026-06-01-S529)
**Status:** OPEN; exact channel audit and proof-language refinement of HYP-2001/HYP-2003.
**What:** A tournament is a binary relation on simplex edges, but LRC-realizable tournaments come from a stricter regular-polygon source: an outside cyclic gap necklace determines hidden inside chord orientations by consecutive gap sums and the half-turn threshold. The dihedral group acts on the outside necklace; inside arcs split into chord-length channels. For LRC, the observer is a clasp vertex, and source is the outside condition that both clasp-adjacent gaps are at least `1/n`; interior runner arcs are shadow data except at boundary walls where diameter/endpoint ties create compactified debt.

A proof of n=14 should force non-AP sweeps to open the outside clasp before they can circulate all hidden endpoint/diameter debt.

## HYP-2010: the apex applies recursively = modular decomposition; H is multiplicative over disjoint apex-modules (oracle-2026-06-01-S531)
**Status:** SUPPORTED (verified); the H-multiplicativity is a candidate THEOREM (proof sketch below).
**What:** Every tile (x,y) is the APEX of the sub-polygon on the contiguous rank-block [y,x], so S530's outside/inside split recurs at every scale. The recursion IS the tournament's MODULAR DECOMPOSITION tree (= recursive sub-ranking tree S520o = recursive triangulation). Three quantities respect it:
 (A) COMBINATORICS: the n-gon contains exactly (n-s+1) sub-polygons of size s (s=3..n), summing to C(n-1,2) tiles -- exact self-similarity.
 (B) H (directed Ham-path count = loneliness meter S26): single apex-flipped block of size s has H=1+2^{s-2} (verified ranges r=2..7 -> 3,5,9,17,33,65). DISJOINT apex-flipped blocks MULTIPLY: H(A+B)=H(A)*H(B) (3*3=9, 5*5=25, verified n=7,8,9) -- because flipped contiguous blocks are MODULES and the transitive quotient has H=1, so H(T)=prod_modules H(module). NESTED flips COUPLE (not multiplicative): concentric "diameter onion" (1,n),(2,n-1),... climbs toward the REGULAR tournament (=regular exactly at n=5, H=15; n=7->123; n=9->1479), endpoint-anchored chain stays low (n=7->31). The disjoint-multiply / nested-couple split is the quantitative form of S520o "arcs are not independent".
 (C) LRC: the feasible (loneliness) arc is carved recursively by runners (cascade S527); carve tree mirrors the sub-polygon tree; #components branches (1,2,4,4,2) then prunes; early shrink ~ (n-2)/n. REGULAR POLYGON carves the apex to EXACTLY 0 (tight); non-AP leaves positive surviving apex. LRC = "recursive carving never empties the observer's apex".
**Proof sketch (B, modular multiplicativity):** contracting each flipped contiguous block (module) to a point yields the transitive tournament (acyclic, H=1); directed Ham paths factor as (paths through quotient) x prod(internal module paths); quotient transitive => unique linear order => H(T)=prod H(module). Candidate THM.
**Predictions:** prove modular H-multiplicativity cleanly (THM); find the nested-coupling generating function along a chain; make the recursive carve rigorous via the three-gap (Steinhaus) theorem for per-runner sub-arc splits.
**See:** `07-reflections/the-apex-recursion-modular-tree-recursive-H-and-recursive-carving-s531.md`, `04-computation/recursive_apex_hierarchy_s531.py` (+.out), `04-computation/recursive_apex_H_law_s531b.py` (+.out), HYP-2008, HYP-2007, S520o, S527, THM-374, THM-382.

## HYP-2011: n=4 parity law -- odd-sum triples have zero inside debt (oracle-2026-06-01-S531)
**Status:** PARTIALLY-TRUE / THM-worthy for the odd-sum branch.
**What:** For n=4, the covering character `g_k=-sin(pi*k/2)/(pi*k)` is supported only on odd `k`. The order-3 inside-debt resonance therefore needs all odd harmonic indices, so reducing mod 2 gives the obstruction `a+b+c=0 mod 2`. Hence `a+b+c` odd implies the inside-debt term is identically zero, and `|SAFE|=1/8+pairwise>0`.
**Evidence:** `04-computation/lrc_n4_parity_inside_debt_s531.py` verifies 0 violations over 1336 primitive triples with speeds <=22; all 752 odd-sum triples have positive safe measure, min `1/18`; the even-sum core contains the unique tight AP `{1,2,3}` in the audited range.
**Predictions:** close the even-sum n=4 core by a mod-4 triple character sum; general n>=6 needs a multi-channel law because the character support has multiple residue classes.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2011-lrc-n4-parity-inside-debt-law.md`, `07-reflections/lrc-n4-parity-law-and-the-loneliness-ladder-s531.md`, `04-computation/lrc_n4_parity_inside_debt_s531.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_n4_parity_inside_debt_s531.out`, HYP-2004, HYP-2007.

## HYP-2012: the LRC multi-channel metric is the independent-pair count floor(n/2) (oracle-2026-06-01-S532)
**Status:** PARTIALLY-TRUE; n=4 fixed-frame claim verified and n=14 CRT channel interpretation supported.
**What:** The multi-channel generalization should track both the amount and state of independent pairs: a maximum matching of disjoint arcs supplies `floor(n/2)` tournament channels, and LRC variants use the corresponding runner matching plus an apex singleton when the moving-runner count is odd. At n=4, with a suitable fixed frame, the two matching-arc bits index all four tournament isomorphism classes; from n=6 onward the obstruction is a joint state of several channels rather than a single parity bit.
**Evidence:** `independent_pairs_channels_s532.py` verifies the n=4 bijection for 8/16 frames, records `floor(n/2)` counts through n=14, and shows n=6 has full-support resonance in all 120 sampled primitive 5-sets. `lrc_independent_pairs_s532.py` identifies n=14 CRT pairs as a maximum runner matching plus apex singleton and decomposes AP resonance debt into within-channel, cross-channel, and higher-order pieces; its tile matching pass was corrected to exact matching so n=8 has 4 channels.
**Predictions:** compute n=6 inside debt as a function of the three-pair joint state; find the mod-6 analogue of the n=4 odd-sum parity law; characterize which n=4 fixed frames give the A000568(4) bijection.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2012-independent-pairs-multichannel.md`, `07-reflections/independent-pairs-are-the-channels-s532.md`, `04-computation/independent_pairs_channels_s532.py`, `04-computation/lrc_independent_pairs_s532.py`, HYP-2011, HYP-2008, HYP-2003.

## HYP-2013: independent pairs determine the iso class exactly iff n<=4; the coupling gap measures the multi-channel remainder (oracle-2026-06-01-S532b)
**Status:** SUPPORTED (verified n=3..8); complement to HYP-2012.
**What:** Flipping an arc-set S (rest fixed) can index all A000568(n) iso classes only if 2^|S| >= A000568(n). INDEPENDENT pairs (a matching) give |S|<=floor(n/2), so they alone determine the iso class iff 2^floor(n/2) >= A000568(n) -- TRUE (equality) iff n<=4. Define COUPLING GAP = ceil(log2 A000568(n)) - floor(n/2) = 0,0,2,3,6,9 for n=3..8 = the genuinely-coupled bits beyond the independent-pair channels. Verified n=5: best 2-arc matching over best frame reaches only 4 of 12 classes (the 2^2 ceiling). Channel gcd-decomposition: skip j -> gcd(n,j) cycles of length n/gcd(n,j); a PERFECT MATCHING (max independence) iff j=n/2 (diameter shell, even n only). n=4 = smallest even n with a diameter matching = the 2 diameters = the user's 2 independent pairs = where the inside is born (S529). Odd n has NO matching channel (all shells are odd cycles, coupled).
**Synthesis:** independent pairs = the FACTORING channels (S531: vertex-disjoint modules multiply H); coupling gap = the nested/shared-vertex obstruction = inside debt (S529) = nested coupling (S531). 'Amount/state of independent pairs' = free coordinate; the gap (>0 for n>=5) = the multi-channel difficulty, zero only n<=4.
**Predictions:** is the coupling gap an achievable bound (floor(n/2) independent + gap coupled arcs index all classes)? does the LRC inside-debt resonance order match the coupling-gap channel count?
**See:** `07-reflections/independent-pairs-determination-boundary-and-coupling-gap-s532b.md`, `04-computation/independent_pairs_boundary_s532b.py` (+.out), HYP-2012 (concurrent independent-pairs=channels), HYP-2010 (modular H), HYP-2007 (inside debt), opus-S524 (n=14 CRT).

## HYP-2014: almost-fixed frame coupling is the multi-channel breakdown (oracle-2026-06-01-S533)
**Status:** SUPPORTED (verified n=3..12 divisibility; clean-frame boundary audited).
**What:** `2^floor(n/2)` divides `A000568(n)` for n=3..12, giving a free pair-flip factor shadow on tournament isomorphism classes. But a clean fixed-frame factorization exists only for n<=4; at n=5 the required frames overlap, so flipping one independent pair changes how the others sit on the shared frame. This breakdown is the inter-pair coupling that makes the LRC multi-channel problem hard.
**Evidence:** `almost_fixed_frame_s533.py` proves the divisibility pattern in the audited range and shows clean fixed frames fail beyond n=4; `n6_inside_debt_three_channel_s533.py` supports the three-channel n=6 gate/apex picture.
**Predictions:** measure the coupling as deviation from the free `(Z/2)^floor(n/2)` product; relate the coupling measure to inside-debt resonance order and to the failure of single-congruence parity laws for n>=6.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2014-almost-fixed-frame-coupling.md`, `07-reflections/almost-fixed-frame-and-the-coupling-s533.md`, `04-computation/almost_fixed_frame_s533.py`, `04-computation/n6_inside_debt_three_channel_s533.py`, HYP-2012, HYP-2013.

## HYP-2015: n=14 diameter-channel state refines the independent-pair metric (codex-2026-06-01-S532)
**Status:** OPEN; K4 toy model and n=14 chord-channel inventory support the refinement.
**What:** HYP-2012 says independent pairs are the multi-channel metric; this refinement says n=14 needs the state and matching shape of those independent pairs under a fixed scaffold. In K4, a fixed scaffold `0->1->2->3` plus `0->3` and two free independent pair-arcs `(0,2),(1,3)` hits all four ordinary tournament isomorphism classes. In clasp-deleted n=14, the diameter channel has exactly six mutually independent hidden chords plus one singleton runner.
**Evidence:** `lrc_independent_pair_channels_s532.py` finds the K4 state table `00,01,10,11` gives the four classes with `(H,c3,SCC)` values `(5,2,(4)), (3,1,(3,1)), (3,1,(3,1)), (1,0,(1,1,1,1))`; 24/48 K4 independent-pair scaffolds are complete coordinates. In n=14, channels `d=1..6` have 12 edges, max matching 6, and 7 maximum matchings, while the diameter channel `d=7` has 6 edges, max matching 6, exactly 1 maximum matching, and all edges independent.
**Predictions:** a wall-only n=14 counterexample must carry a compatible state vector on the six diameter independent pairs; if that vector is not AP-balanced, the outside clasp opens or endpoint/resonance debt exports to a deeper labelled layer. "Wall-only => AP" should factor through a finite classifier of diameter-pair states under fixed non-diameter scaffold.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2015-lrc-diameter-channel-state.md`, `07-reflections/lrc-independent-pair-channel-state-s532.md`, `04-computation/lrc_independent_pair_channels_s532.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_independent_pair_channels_s532.out`, HYP-2009, HYP-2011, HYP-2012, HYP-2013, HYP-2014.

## HYP-2016: the mod-6 three-channel parity law for the n=6 inside debt (oracle-2026-06-01-S533c)
**Status:** VERIFIED (computation, n6_full_support_three_channel_law_s533b.py); generalizes the n=4 'a+b+c odd' parity law.
**What:** In the covering expansion |LONELY|=sum_{sum m_i v_i=0} prod ghat(m_i), the characters vanish iff n*|m where n*=n/2 (even n) or n (odd n). A full-support resonance (all n-1 runners) needs, mod n*, that 0 = sum u_i v_i for units u_i in (Z/n*)^x. 
 n=4 (n*=2, units {1}): one fixed sum -> debt-free iff sum v_i ODD = 'a+b+c odd' (ONE channel, no sign freedom).
 n=6 (n*=3, units {+-1}): a SIGN per runner -> debt-free iff NO eps in {+-1}^5 has sum eps_i v_i = 0 mod 3. THE FIRST genuinely multi-channel parity law. mod 6 = (mod-3 residue channel) (x) (mod-2 sign-unit). The 3 channels = residues mod 3 = the diameter/antipodal pairs (S530/S532).
**Reduction (verified):** inert runners (v=0 mod3) contribute 0; active runners (v!=0 mod3) contribute {1,2} mod3 freely; with k=#active the reachable sums mod3 = all of Z/3 iff k>=2, {1,2} iff k=1. So FULL-SUPPORT debt VANISHES iff k=1 (for primitive sets: exactly ONE runner !=0 mod3). Verified on curated sets ((3,6,9,12,1) etc. debt=0, zero resonances) and 400/401 random primitive 5-sets (1 miss = bounded-search integer-feasibility edge case).
**Value vs feasibility:** existence is mod 3; the debt VALUE/sign is graded by m mod 6 (|ghat(m)|=sqrt3/(2pi m), sign by m mod 6: 1,2->-, 4,5->+). 'Any-order' debt is almost always present (inert runners form a scaled 3*(...) sub-system with its own resonances = the S531 recursion); the CLEAN channel law is the full-support term, exactly as n=4's order>=3 IS full-support.
**Unified (all n):** full-support debt-free iff 0 not representable as sum (units mod n*) v_i; #channels = #residue classes, per-runner freedom = (Z/n*)^x. 'a+b+c odd' = the unit-trivial shadow; n=6 = first nontrivial unit group.
**Predictions:** n=8 (n*=4, units {1,3}) signed-by-{1,3} balance mod 4; n=9 (n*=9, units phi(9)=6) six-fold unit channel; tie integer-feasibility edge cases to the resonance lattice.
**See:** `07-reflections/n6-inside-debt-three-channel-parity-law-mod6-s533c.md`, `04-computation/n6_full_support_three_channel_law_s533b.py` (+.out), `04-computation/n6_inside_debt_three_channel_s533.py` (+.out), HYP-2007 (inside debt), HYP-2012/2013 (independent pairs=channels), HYP-2014/2015 (concurrent S533/S532), S531 (n=4 parity), S526 (mod-3 Legendre).

## HYP-2017: LRC n=18 attempt -- parity obstruction is VACUOUS at n*=9 (potent only at n=4); the prime-power degradation (oracle-2026-06-01-S534o)
**Status:** honest proof ATTEMPT (n=18 NOT proved); rigorous sub-cases + new parity-degradation finding.
**What:** n=18 (17 speeds, 1/18), n*=n/2=9=3^2. (1) RIGOROUS (THM-369 sieve): for every q<=18 with no speed divisible by q, t=1/q is 18-lonely (1/q>=1/18); verified 245/245 no-mult-of-9 sets lonely at t=1/9. A counterexample must be q-COVERED for all q in {2..18}. (2) NEW: the S533c parity law generalizes (full-support debt-free iff 0 not representable as sum c_i v_i mod n*, c_i in {1..n*-1}) and is VACUOUS at n=18: 0/3000 primitive 17-sets debt-free. Firing-rate degradation: n=4(n*=2,units{1}) ~60%; n=6(n*=3) ~3%; n>=8 0%. The parity obstruction is essentially UNIQUE to n=4 (trivial unit group). 3-adic reason: mod 9 nonzero residues split into units {1,2,4,5,7,8} (v3=0) and {3,6} (v3=1); a v3=1 runner can contribute 0 mod9 (c=3,6), a unit runner contributes all {1..8}; so any unit runner => 0 reachable => debt present. Debt-free needs ALL runners div by 3 => recurses to n=6 mod-3 law one 3-adic level down (non-primitive) = the prime-power recursion. (3) HEURISTIC: cascade threshold (n-1)((n-2)/n)^{n-2}=17*(8/9)^16=2.58>=1 passes (margins 1.12/2.04/2.31/2.58 for n=7/14/16/18); AP=regular-18gon carves to EXACTLY 0 (tight extremal), random primitive stays ~0.148; non-rigorous (Erdos-Turan discrepancy log factor).
**Verdict:** n=18 NOT proved. Wall = sieve-covered residual + discrepancy/coupling gap (same as n=14/16, general conjecture). Parity route now known insufficient beyond n=4.
**Predictions:** closing n=18 needs either (i) rigorous discrepancy bound for the cascade shrink on the sieve-covered residual, or (ii) coupled control of the order>=3 inside debt the parity bit cannot see.
**See:** `07-reflections/lrc-n18-proof-attempt-parity-vacuity-prime-power-s534o.md`, `04-computation/lrc_n18_proof_attempt_s534.py` (+.out), HYP-2016 (mod-6 three-channel parity), HYP-2007 (inside debt), HYP-2003 (n14 covering), S527 (cascade), THM-369 (sieve), S17.

## HYP-2018: LRC mapping spectrum -- restriction of realizable iso-classes = retained metric; the NEAR-GRAPH mapping is the sweet spot (oracle-2026-06-01-S535o)
**Status:** SUPPORTED (computed n=4,5,6); reframes LRC as membership on a restricted family.
**What:** The S518 order-tournament menu is non-restrictive (the conjecture hides in the walk) because a tournament keeps only ORDER; loneliness is METRIC (1/n). PRINCIPLE: the realizable iso-class set shrinks as the mapping retains more metric. Computed spectrum (R=realizable/all):
 - M0 order tournament: no restriction (walk).
 - M1 NEAR-GRAPH (i~j iff circular dist<1/n): realizable = circular indifference graphs; R=8/11,20/34,54/156=0.73,0.59,0.35 (SHRINKS with n). LRC@n <=> the marked OBSERVER-ISOLATED near-graph is in EVERY speed set's realizable family. Verified 120/121,120/121,60/61 -- the lone misses are the tight AP/regular-polygon set (lonely only at measure-zero t=k/n the grid skips) = the boundary extremal.
 - M2 3-level metric tournament (near/mid/far): realizable 14 (n=4), 75 (n=5) vs 3^6=729, 3^10=59049 -- near-total restriction; target = circular-metric/Robinson colorings.
 - M3 QR/Paley tournament (i->j iff v_i-v_j is QR mod n): collapses to ~1 Paley-type class (R=0.25 n=5, 0.018 n=7); CAVEAT: n=1 mod4 makes QR symmetric (degenerate), needs n=3 mod4.
**Lesson:** realizable-class restriction is meaningful exactly to the extent the 1/n metric is carried; near-graph (1 threshold bit over the order tournament) is the sweet spot turning LRC into membership on a shrinking family.
**Predictions:** realizable near-graphs are EXACTLY circular indifference graphs; characterize the observer-isolable subfamily; restriction R->0 as n->inf.
**See:** `07-reflections/lrc-mapping-spectrum-metric-retention-restricts-the-realizable-classes-s535o.md`, `04-computation/lrc_mappings_restriction_spectrum_s535.py` (+.out), HYP-2007 (inside debt), HYP-2008 (apex=largest gap), S518/S519 (menu/walk).

## HYP-2019: the multitude of metric/arithmetic LRC mappings (p-adic, nerve, wiring, matroid, CF, Robinson) -- restricted realizability (oracle-2026-06-01-S535o)
**Status:** OPEN (conjectural family; posed for investigation).
**What:** Beyond the computed M1-M3, a multitude of mappings each retaining a different metric/arithmetic slice, each conjectured to give a restricted realizable iso-class set with LRC as a membership/feature statement:
 - MAP-p: edge label v_p(v_i-v_j), p|n* (prime-power filtration, S534) -> ULTRAMETRIC patterns (every triangle isosceles); LRC reads the deepest valuation; S533/S534 channels = quotients.
 - MAP-nerve: Cech complex of the danger cover {B_i={t:||v_i t||<1/n}}; LRC <=> nerve does NOT cover [0,1) (H_0 of complement survives); restricted by Helly-on-circle/periodic-arc.
 - MAP-wire: cyclic ordering sequence = closed ALLOWABLE SEQUENCE (wiring diagram of runner lines); realizable = STRETCHABLE; LRC = sequence has an observer-isolated (wide-gap) frame.
 - MAP-matroid: resonance-independence matroid of the speeds (Q-representable); LRC couples to matroid connectivity / inside-debt support (S533).
 - MAP-CF: speed-ratio continued fractions; three-gap (Steinhaus) governs; LRC = largest gap (apex S530) stays >=2/n along the CF descent.
 - MAP-Robinson: time-averaged closeness = circular Robinson(ian) matrix; LRC = observer row uniformly far.
**Predictions:** each realizable family is a classified restricted class (ultrametric/circular-arc/stretchable/representable-matroid/bounded-CF/circular-Robinsonian); the MOST restrictive that still has LRC=membership wins. MAP-p ties directly to the S534 n=18 prime-power channels; MAP-wire to S519/S520 walk.
**See:** `07-reflections/lrc-mapping-spectrum-metric-retention-restricts-the-realizable-classes-s535o.md`, HYP-2018, HYP-2017 (n=18 prime-power), HYP-2008 (apex), S521o (permutohedron).
## HYP-2020: LRC lives in a restricted tournament quotient stack (codex-2026-06-01-S535)
**Status:** OPEN; bounded exact audit plus abstract proof-language proposal.
**What:** The A000568 formulation should be sharpened from one quotient to a stack of restricted tournament-class maps, complementary to HYP-2018/HYP-2019's metric-retention spectrum: raw circular body -> source-deleted target -> observer-source marked class -> gap/apex threshold fiber -> kinetic gap-flow class -> endpoint blocker-deficit class -> labelled endpoint-pressure/channel state. The proof question remains "which isomorphism classes can the arithmetic clock exhibit?", but the class universe is a small marked/thresholded/kinetic image rather than all raw tournament classes.
**Evidence:** `lrc_restricted_tournament_mappings_s535.py` audits exact wall/open-cell samples for primitive clocks at total `n=4..7`. Raw phase classes remain mixed (`2/2`, `4/4`, `11/11`, `7/17` mixed), while source-deleted target classes certify all sampled systems with images `2,4,11,9`. Threshold-decorated maps (`observer_source_marked`, `gap_threshold_necklace`, `gap_kinetic_flow`, `blocker_deficit_shadow`, `apex_boundary_runner`) have zero mixed fibers and certify every sampled speed set. The map meta-tournament is transitive with `source_deleted_phase` first and raw `phase_runner` last.
**Predictions:** the source-deleted target image should equal HYP-1999's Ferrers interval menu plus THM-383 boundary classes; kinetic gap-flow should become strictly smaller than static gap-threshold as THM-387 transition restrictions accumulate; hard frontiers such as `n=14` should appear as finite intersections of source target, apex-boundary target, HYP-2015 diameter-channel state, and endpoint-pressure debt.
**See:** `05-knowledge/hypotheses/HYP-2020-lrc-restricted-tournament-quotient-stack.md`, `04-computation/lrc_restricted_tournament_mappings_s535.py`, `05-knowledge/results/lrc_restricted_tournament_mappings_s535.out`, `07-reflections/lrc-restricted-tournament-quotient-stack-s535.md`, HYP-1981, HYP-1982, HYP-1986, HYP-1988, HYP-1999, HYP-2001, HYP-2008, HYP-2009, HYP-2015, HYP-2018, HYP-2019.
